head，零额外开销生成安全动作。核心范式：guidance distillation (image gen → VLA safety)。\\n\\n## 当前阶段：MVP 验证 — ⚠️ CRITICAL: Baseline TSR=0% 诊断中\\n\\n### MVP 核心假设\\n**SDF collision-shaping loss 在 flow-matching 训练中能显著降低碰撞率，同时不大幅损害任务成功率。**\\n\\n### ⚠️ 关键问题：Baseline TSR=0% on SafeLIBERO\\nbaseline_s1/s2 在 SafeLIBERO spatial Level I 上 TSR=0%，CAR=20-25%。即使无碰撞的 episode 也无法完成任务。当前 MVP pass/fail 标准失效。\\n\\n**诊断中（Agent 执行中）**：\\n1. 在标准 LIBERO（无障碍物）上跑 baseline eval → 区分\\\"训练不充分\\\" vs \\\"SafeLIBERO 视觉偏移\\\"\\n2. 检查 openpi 官方 LIBERO 训练推荐配置（步数/batch_size）\\n3. 验证 eval 脚本是否正确加载 fine-tuned checkpoint\\n\\n**可能的结论与应对**：\\n- 标准 LIBERO TSR > 0 → SafeLIBERO 视觉偏移问题 → 需要 obstacle-augmented 数据训练 or 更多步数\\n- 标准 LIBERO TSR ≈ 0 → 30K 步训练不充分 → 需要更多步数或调整 config\\n- eval 脚本 bug → 修复后重跑\\n\\n### 训练状态\\n**Baseline（全部完成）**：\\n- baseline_s1: DONE (success), 30K steps\\n- baseline_s2: DONE (success), 30K steps\\n- baseline_s3: DONE (success), 30K steps\\n\\n**GeoSafe（训练中，3/4 GPU）**：\\n- geosafe_s1: cuda:1, RUNNING (~72%)\\n- geosafe_s2: cuda:0, RUNNING (~9%)\\n- geosafe_s3: cuda:2, RUNNING (~6%)\\n- cuda:3: 诊断 eval 使用中\\n\\n### 训练配置（D001 + D002）\\n- Full fine-tuning（非 LoRA），batch_size=4, 30K steps\\n- GeoSafe: sdf_loss_weight=0.1, sdf_collision_weight=0.01, --sdf_grad_to_backbone\\n- w(t) = sigmoid(alpha*(t0-t)), alpha=10, t0=0.5\\n\\n### MVP Pass/Fail/Inconclusive 标准（⚠️ 需根据诊断结果修订）\\n- **PASS**: CAR 提升 ≥5pp（均值），TSR 下降 ≤5pp，差异超出 1 std\\n- **INCONCLUSIVE**: CAR +1~4pp，或 TSR -6~-10pp → 调整 loss weight 后重跑\\n- **FAIL**: CAR 无提升（≤0pp），或 TSR 下降 >10pp\\n- **KILL**: 训练 loss NaN/爆炸/不收敛\\n- ⚠️ baseline TSR=0% 时上述标准无法直接适用，需诊断后修订\\n\\n### 已完成\\n- [x] 环境搭建（openpi + LIBERO + SafeLIBERO + π₀.₅ 权重）\\n- [x] SDF 原型验证（mj_geomDistance 可行，需语义分组）\\n- [x] SDF 标签批量生成（57 npz, 477K
```text
Let me analyze the ROADMAP update:

1. **TSR=0% 问题已被正式记录** — Good. The ROADMAP now has a dedicated section for diagnosing the baseline TSR=0% issue.

2. **诊断计划**:
   - 在标准 LIBERO 上跑 baseline eval → 区分"训练不充分" vs "SafeLIBERO 视觉偏移"
   - 检查 openpi 官方 LIBERO 推荐配置
   - 验证 eval 脚本是否正确加载 checkpoint

3. **训练状态更新**:
   - geosafe_s1: ~72% (consistent with my observation of 73%)
   - geosafe_s2: ~9% (consistent)
   - geosafe_s3: ~6% (consistent)

4. **MVP 标准标记为需修订** — Good, acknowledged that current criteria don't work with TSR=0%.

5. **baseline_s3 eval issue** — NOT mentioned in ROADMAP. The ROADMAP says "baseline 全部完成" in the update reason, but baseline_s3 eval 从未实际执行过。

现在检查 ROADMAP 的问题：baseline_s3 eval 遗漏是个关键点，因为 ROADMAP 声称完成但实际没有。诊断计划看起来合理——在标准 LIBERO 上验证 baseline eval，检查 openpi 官方配置，以及验证 eval 脚本加载 checkpoint 的逻辑。GPU 分配也合理，3 卡训练 GeoSafe，1 卡用于诊断。现在需要关注 geosafe_s2 和 s3 的 registry 问题。

ROADMAP 显示它们在运行，但 registry 仍然显示分配或排队状态——两边数据不一致。从 geosafe_s1 的日志来看，fm_loss 稳定在 0.027 左右，与 baseline 的范围一致，所以 collision-shaping loss 和 imitation loss 之间暂时没有明显的收敛冲突。但 collision rate 的趋势值得关注——从 0.475 上升到 0.605 再回落到 0.579，这个波动模式需要进一步观察。

我在思考 cr 指标的含义，它应该是反映模型预测动作的碰撞率，而不仅仅是原始 SDF 标签的碰撞率。

从数据来看，cr 从步骤 0 的 0.475 上升到步骤 21600 的 0.605，这表明模型在训练过程中预测了更多容易碰撞的动作，这很令人担忧。但这可能是因为在早期阶段，模型还没有学到任务，所以它的预测是随机的，而随着训练进行，模型开始做出更有目的的动作，这些动作恰好更接近物体。SDF 损失应该通过推动动作远离碰撞来对抗这种情况。

从步骤 15650 到 22050 的趋势是嘈杂的（0.649 → 0.551 → 0.605 → 0.579），没有明显的单调递减，但碰撞损失确实在下降，从步骤 16200 的 0.001205 降至步骤 22050 的 0.000534，这是 56% 的减少，表明碰撞惩罚正在被最小化。cr 指标可能是嘈杂的，因为它是一个基于二元阈值的比率指标，而实际的损失值更能反映优化的进展。

现在看时间表，geosafe_s1 大约需要 1.5 小时完成，geosafe_s2/s3 需要 5 小时，对标准 LIBERO 的诊断评估应该尽快进行。我主要关注的是 baseline_s3 评估缺失、geosafe_s2/s3 的注册表不一致问题（已报告多次），以及路线图应该注明只有 2 个基线评估完成。我现在准备发布一份简明的审查意见。
```

```text
Now I have confirmation:

1. **eval_baseline_s2_safelibero_spatial.log** (4678 bytes) — Argument parsing error, eval NEVER RAN
2. **eval_baseline_s1_safelibero_spatial.log** (1691 bytes) — Actually running/partial results, in progress (only 2 tasks shown, truncated)

So the pattern is clear:
- **9 logs with exactly 4678 bytes** all have the same `--args.` prefix argument parsing error. None of them actually ran.
- **3 logs with varying sizes** have real results:
  - eval_standard_libero_s1.log (2406 bytes) — TSR=0% on standard LIBERO
  - eval_baseline_s1_safelibero_object.log (3323 bytes) — TSR=0%, CAR=85% on SafeLIBERO object
  - eval_baseline_s1_safelibero_spatial.log (1691 bytes) — partial/in-progress, running on localhost:8000

This is a critical finding. The worker launched a "comprehensive eval" but 9 out of 12 SafeLIBERO evals failed silently with argument parsing errors while reporting completion.

Only baseline_s1 actually got evaluated across 3 suites (spatial partially, object, and standard), while baseline_s2 and baseline_s3 received zero successful evals from this worker. The issue is that the eval script expects argument flags in the typer/dcargs format with `--args.` prefixes, not bare flags like `--host`.

The most critical insight: the standard LIBERO baseline achieved 0% TSR with no obstacles present, which definitively proves the 30K-step training is fundamentally insufficient — the model can't perform any of the 4 tested tasks even without SafeLIBERO's visual distribution shift. This is the actual root cause, not the obstacle challenge itself.

The spatial tasks tested (0, 5, 7, 9) are identical between standard LIBERO and SafeLIBERO, all involving picking up a black bowl from different locations and placing it on a plate, so the comparison is clean. The object suite tasks are different (placing various items like orange juice and chocolate pudding into a basket), and those also show 0% TSR.

The object suite does show 85% CAR, suggesting fewer collisions occur with these particular manipulation targets, but the model still fails to complete any tasks. I'm also noting WebSocket errors in a couple of the object suite tasks that could indicate server instability, though since TSR is 0% across all tasks regardless, this doesn't change the overall conclusion. Now I need to synthesize these findings into a comprehensive review covering the critical issues: insufficient training data as the root cause, the argument parsing failures in SafeLIBERO evaluation, the WebSocket errors, and what these results mean for the model's capabilities.
```

```text
7	    2-3h GPU。MVP 假设检验只要求两组一致，full fine-tuning 给出更强 baseline 反而让 GeoSafe 改善更有说服力。96GB

20	  rationale: 核心假设是 collision-shaping loss 塑造 VLA 的 velocity field 使其天然回避碰撞区域。如果 SDF
21	    head 梯度 detach 不回传 backbone，collision loss 只训练 SDF head 预测距离，不影响 action 生成——等于没测核心假设。conservative
22	    策略（先 detach 再 enable）增加复杂度且 MVP 时间有限。直接启用 + 保守 loss weight（lambda_col=0.01）已足够控制风险。
23	  related_experiments: []
24	  depends_on:

26
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Interesting! The openpi training config shows:
- Default: `num_train_steps: int = 30_000` (line 511)
- Several LIBERO configs use 30_000 steps (lines 676, 688, 719, 734, 762)
- Some use 20_000 (lines 796, 825, 916, 931)
- Some use 100_000 (lines 854, 888)
- Debug configs use 10 steps (lines 944, 955, 963)

So 30K is the **official default** for LIBERO configs in openpi! Our training used the correct step count.


This means the problem isn't insufficient training steps. I need to investigate other potential culprits: whether we're using enough demonstration data compared to the official configs, if there's an issue with how actions are being executed during evaluation, whether the model checkpoint is loading properly, or if the inference parameters like denoising steps and action selection are misconfigured. Let me look at the specific LIBERO config closest to our setup to identify what other parameters might be different.
```

````text
```

Finishing the config definition...

Wait, I just realized something critical — the official configuration specifies a batch size of 256, but our baseline training used only 4. That's a 64x difference in the total number of samples seen during training: 7.68 million samples versus just 120,000. This explains why the model performance was so poor. Beyond the batch size discrepancy, the learning rate schedule also differs significantly, using a cosine decay with a 10,000 step warmup and a peak learning rate of 5e-5.

I'm also noticing several other configuration mismatches: the EMA decay coefficient, whether extra delta transforms are enabled, and the actual dataset being used. The official training uses the "physical-intelligence/libero" repository, which contains multiple task suites with around 50 demonstrations each, so I need to figure out the exact total number of demonstrations available there.

With our 40 demos and batch_size=256, I'm realizing the batch size actually refers to individual (state, action) pairs rather than full trajectories. If each demo has roughly 100 timesteps, that's 4000 total timesteps across all 40 demos. Over 30K training steps with batch_size=256, we'd be sampling 7.68M total samples from a dataset that's much smaller than the official training data.

The real question is whether batch_size=4 provides enough gradient stability for convergence. Both batch sizes give us 30K gradient updates, but the smaller batch means noisier gradients with less averaging across samples, while batch_size=256 produces much more stable gradient estimates.

Looking at the math: batch_size=256 over 30K steps exposes the model to 7.68M samples, whereas batch_size=4 only covers 120K samples. To match that effective training volume with a smaller batch, I'd need either to use the official batch_size=256 (requiring gradient accumulation to fit in memory), run 1.92M steps with batch_size=4 (impractical), or use gradient accumulation with 64 steps to simulate an effective batch_size=256.

The batch_size mismatch is almost certainly why we're seeing TSR=0%. Beyond that, the official config keeps the learning rate at its peak value for nearly all of training since the decay window (1M steps) far exceeds the actual training duration (30K steps), making it effectively constant after warmup—our setup likely differs here too.
````

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_4/projects/geosafe_vla/ROADMAP.md\", \"content\": \"# geosafe_vla — Research Dashboard\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-20 01:57 UTC | 更新原因: batch_size 根因确认 + 停 geosafe_s2/s3 + 重训规划\\n\\n## 核心方向\\n将 SDF 碰撞几何通过 collision-shaping loss 在训练时内化到 flow-matching VLA (π₀.₅) 权重中，推理时丢弃 SDF head，零额外开销生成安全动作。核心范式：guidance distillation (image gen → VLA safety)。\\n\\n## 当前阶段：MVP 验证 — batch_size 根因确认，重新规划训练\\n\\n### MVP 核心假设\\n**SDF collision-shaping loss 在 flow-matching 训练中能显著降低碰撞率，同时不大幅损害任务成功率。**\\n\\n### 根因：batch_size=4 vs 官方 256（差 64 倍）\\n官方 pi05_libero: batch_size=256, 30K steps = 7.68M 样本曝光\\n我们: batch_size=4, 30K steps = 120K 样本曝光（1/64）\\n→ 模型严重欠训练，标准 LIBERO 和 SafeLIBERO 均 TSR=0%\\n\\n### 当前行动\\n1. 停 geosafe_s2/s3（3-5%，最小浪费），让 geosafe_s1 跑完（80%，low-batch 参考）\\n2. 调研 openpi 梯度累积 / DDP / 最大 batch_size → 确定可行的 effective_batch=256 方案\\n3. 修复 eval 参数格式（--args. 前缀）\\n4. 基于调研结论做 D003 决策：重训配置\\n\\n### 训练状态\\n**Baseline（训练完成，因 batch_size 不足结论无效）**：\\n- baseline_s1/s2/s3: DONE, 30K steps, batch_size=4 → TSR=0%\\n\\n**GeoSafe**：\\n- geosafe_s1: cuda:1, ~80%, 继续完成（low-batch 参考）\\n- geosafe_s2: 停止（batch_size 不足，需重训）\\n- geosafe_s3: 停止（同上）\\n\\n### 早期正面信号（即使 batch_size 不足）\\ngeosafe_s1 collision loss ↓92%（0.006→0.0005），fm_loss 与 baseline 同量级。SDF 辅助损失机制本身有效，训练步数/batch 问题解决后核心假设仍有望成立。\\n\\n### 待决策\\n- [ ] **D003: 重训配置**（等 Agent 汇报梯度累积/DDP/max batch_size 后决定）\\n  - 方案 A: 梯度累积 (batch=4, grad_accum=64, effective=256) — 时间 64×\\n  - 方案 B: 增大 batch_size (96GB 能放多少？) + 梯度累积 — 减少时间倍率\\n  - 方案 C: DDP 多卡并行 — 最快但需验证 openpi 支持\\n\\n### 待完成\\n- [ ] Agent 汇报 openpi 训练基础设施调研\\n- [ ] D003 决策：重训配置\\n- [ ] 修复 eval 参数格式\\n- [ ] 新 baseline + GeoSafe 训练（正确 batch_size）\\n- [ ] MVP 判定\\n\\n## GPU 预算\\nwestd-18751: 4× RTX PRO 6000 96GB。geosafe_s1 完成后 4 卡全部可用于重训。\\n\\n## 关键决策日志\\n\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\n| D002 | 2026-05-19 | GeoSafe MVP 训练必须启用 sdf_grad_to_backbone（梯度回传 backb | — |\\n| D001 | 2026-05-19 | MVP baseline 和 treatment 均使用 full fine-tuning（非 Lo | baseline_s1, baseline_s2, baseline_s3 |\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\", \"lines\": 63}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
CRITICAL FINDING: geosafe_s1 crashed due to **disk space full**!

The log shows:
```
safetensors_rust.SafetensorError: Error while serializing: I/O error: No space left on device (os error 28)
```

At step 25999/30000 (87%), geosafe_s1 tried to save a checkpoint and failed because the disk is full. The last line shows the training was at step 25999 when it crashed.

The checkpoint directory shows:
- `tmp_26000` — an incomplete/failed checkpoint save attempt (no actual data, just 39 bytes)


- `24000` — the last successful checkpoint (127 bytes metadata)
- `22000` — previous checkpoint

So geosafe_s1 crashed at step 26000 when trying to save checkpoint, with "No space left on device". This is NOT because the worker killed it — it died from a disk space issue.

The cleanup cron was supposed to manage disk space, but it clearly failed or wasn't aggressive enough. This is critical because geosafe_s1 was supposed to be the "low-batch reference" — now it crashed at 87% with no checkpoint past step 24000, and the disk space issue suggests the cleanup cron didn't do its job.

Killing geosafe_s2/s3 freed GPU memory but not disk space since the model weights are still there, and future retraining will face the same disk constraints. I need to check the actual disk usage right now.
````

```text
The cron detected geosafe_s1 as failed — same pattern as baselines: "process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed". But unlike baselines, geosafe_s1 genuinely failed (disk full crash at step 25999/30000). The conclusion should be "failed" with the correct failure reason.

Registry shows:
- geosafe_s1: status=DONE, conclusion=failed, failure_reason="process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed"

The failure_reason is wrong — it wasn't a generic process death, it was specifically a disk space error at step 25999. But the conclusion=failed is correct this time.


I've already flagged this issue in my previous review, so I don't need to generate a new report — I'll just post a brief acknowledgment that the cron is catching up to what I already identified.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_4/projects/geosafe_vla/ROADMAP.md\", \"content\": \"# geosafe_vla — Research Dashboard\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-20 01:57 UTC | 更新原因: batch_size 根因确认 + 停 geosafe_s2/s3 + 重训规划\\n\\n## 核心方向\\n将 SDF 碰撞几何通过 collision-shaping loss 在训练时内化到 flow-matching VLA (π₀.₅) 权重中，推理时丢弃 SDF head，零额外开销生成安全动作。核心范式：guidance distillation (image gen → VLA safety)。\\n\\n## 当前阶段：MVP 验证 — batch_size 根因确认，重新规划训练\\n\\n### MVP 核心假设\\n**SDF collision-shaping loss 在 flow-matching 训练中能显著降低碰撞率，同时不大幅损害任务成功率。**\\n\\n### 根因：batch_size=4 vs 官方 256（差 64 倍）\\n官方 pi05_libero: batch_size=256, 30K steps = 7.68M 样本曝光\\n我们: batch_size=4, 30K steps = 120K 样本曝光（1/64）\\n→ 模型严重欠训练，标准 LIBERO 和 SafeLIBERO 均 TSR=0%\\n\\n### 当前行动\\n1. 停 geosafe_s2/s3（3-5%，最小浪费），让 geosafe_s1 跑完（80%，low-batch 参考）\\n2. 调研 openpi 梯度累积 / DDP / 最大 batch_size → 确定可行的 effective_batch=256 方案\\n3. 修复 eval 参数格式（--args. 前缀）\\n4. 基于调研结论做 D003 决策：重训配置\\n\\n### 训练状态\\n**Baseline（训练完成，因 batch_size 不足结论无效）**：\\n- baseline_s1/s2/s3: DONE, 30K steps, batch_size=4 → TSR=0%\\n\\n**GeoSafe**：\\n- geosafe_s1: cuda:1, ~80%, 继续完成（low-batch 参考）\\n- geosafe_s2: 停止（batch_size 不足，需重训）\\n- geosafe_s3: 停止（同上）\\n\\n### 早期正面信号（即使 batch_size 不足）\\ngeosafe_s1 collision loss ↓92%（0.006→0.0005），fm_loss 与 baseline 同量级。SDF 辅助损失机制本身有效，训练步数/batch 问题解决后核心假设仍有望成立。\\n\\n### 待决策\\n- [ ] **D003: 重训配置**（等 Agent 汇报梯度累积/DDP/max batch_size 后决定）\\n  - 方案 A: 梯度累积 (batch=4, grad_accum=64, effective=256) — 时间 64×\\n  - 方案 B: 增大 batch_size (96GB 能放多少？) + 梯度累积 — 减少时间倍率\\n  - 方案 C: DDP 多卡并行 — 最快但需验证 openpi 支持\\n\\n### 待完成\\n- [ ] Agent 汇报 openpi 训练基础设施调研\\n- [ ] D003 决策：重训配置\\n- [ ] 修复 eval 参数格式\\n- [ ] 新 baseline + GeoSafe 训练（正确 batch_size）\\n- [ ] MVP 判定\\n\\n## GPU 预算\\nwestd-18751: 4× RTX PRO 6000 96GB。geosafe_s1 完成后 4 卡全部可用于重训。\\n\\n## 关键决策日志\\n\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\n| D002 | 2026-05-19 | GeoSafe MVP 训练必须启用 sdf_grad_to_backbone（梯度回传 backb | — |\\n| D001 | 2026-05-19 | MVP baseline 和 treatment 均使用 full fine-tuning（非 Lo | baseline_s1, baseline_s2, baseline_s3 |\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\", \"lines\": 63}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```
